Fonte: design/tripo/mago-roteiro-tripo.md

Mago: roteiro Tripo do piloto ao nível 150 (copia-e-cola)

Passada de personalidade 19/11 (régua nº 1 do criador: "hoje TUDO é visual"; nível herói Lost Ark / Diablo IV desde o nível 1).
- Identidade nova: runista jovem do cristal, não o velho mago genérico. Silhueta sino alto (barra em sino, mangas enormes, gola alta dura),
assinatura runas de ouro na barra + rune-book pendurado no quadril, cabeça fixa diadema astral com runa-estrela (nunca chapéu, nunca capuz).
- Contraste: celeste fosco dominante + marfim #EFE6D2 como terceiro tom claro (forros e sub-roupão) + ouro vivo + magenta pequeno. Proibido monocromia, azul-marinho/índigo/roxo/preto dominante (Necromante, Sombra) e creme dominante (Oráculo).
- Piloto: um conceito por nó (sem folha de 4); MULHER com corte feminino real (cláusula FEMALE CUT no 1-15, BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES do 21 em diante).
- 1-60 reescritos: intermediários com ≥2 marcas de longe (tubo de pergaminhos 6, painéis marfim 15, mangas marfim e gola-livro 26, astrolábio 37, orrery 44-A, aro de tear 44-B, cristal 48, orrery 55-A, dois aros 55-B); BULK nos intermediários; 60 com SHOWPIECE + ARCH-MAGE ACCENTS.
- Corrigido: o 60 "obsidiana índigo escuro" (viraria Necromante e o Mago S6 já saiu "vinho" na prova de distância) passa a celeste com obsidiana só de acento; capas "night-blue" do 121 e 131 passam a celeste forradas de azul-noite.
- 61-150: só o bloco de identidade trocado. Nada foi gerado.
Status: pronto para colar, 19/11 (padronização final: níveis 1 a 37 completos; 41 a 150 em modelo de ramos). Nada foi gerado. Autor: Claude (art-director). Modelo: design/tripo/barbaro-cadeia-armaduras-1-150.md (mesmos níveis, chaves e intermediárias, modelo de ramos do 41 ao 150: ITEM neutro, HOMEM e MULHER por ramo, ARMA única).
Fontes: design/art/visual-equipamento-por-classe.md (§3, §4, §10, §11), design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md §1.6, design/narrative/historias-das-classes-v2.md §6 (runista do Alto das Runas; o aro leva a runa de "firme" e ganha uma runa por série). Entrada só de imagens nossas (nunca Asset Store nem Fab).
Ramos: A = Arcanista (40) → Cristalomante (60) → Prisma Ardente (80) → Senhor da Tempestade (100) → Arconte de Cristal (130): elementos e reações. B = Tecelão (40) → Mestre dos Fios (60) → Fio-Mestre (80) → Tecelão do Veio (100) → Fio Primordial (130): fios, urdidura e teia.

0. Resumo (5 linhas)

  1. Identidade: runista jovem do cristal (Ironroot, Alto das Runas), elegante e afiado; família Roupão, sem metal pesado; do 41 em diante, capa longa (estilo Diablo IV).
  2. Cor e contraste: azul-celeste #3E86C6 fosco e saturado (45-55%) + marfim #EFE6D2 (15-20%, forros e sub-roupão: o tom claro que impede o borrão azul) + ouro vivo #D8A93A (runas, estrela, espiral) + pequeno magenta #A8326E.
  3. Silhueta-assinatura: sino alto: barra em sino, mangas em sino enormes (forro marfim visível), gola alta dura que vira leque/livro aberto e, do 44, peça de costas (orrery no A, aro de tear no B). Detalhe-assinatura: runas de ouro na barra + rune-book no quadril, que cresce de livrinho a tomo.
  4. Peça de cabeça fixa: diadema astral com runa-estrela (faixa no 1; arco, crescente, coroa de pontas, cristal até o 150); cabelo e rosto sempre visíveis, nunca chapéu nem capuz.
  5. Arma: cajado com orbe/cristal (A: orbe, glifos e orrery; B: tear, fusos e fios; do 41 a arma mistura os dois). Só o nível 150 tem brilho sutil.

MAGE IDENTITY (bloco fixo; vai dentro de TODO prompt de HOMEM e MULHER, antes de "OUTFIT:"): `` MAGE IDENTITY (never lose this): a young rune-scholar of the crystal, elegant, sharp and arcane, NOT a generic old wizard. SILHOUETTE: a tall bell: an ankle-length robe flaring into a wide bell hem, HUGE bell sleeves far wider than the elbows and a tall stiff standing collar framing the head. SIGNATURE: gold runes stitched along the hem and sleeve edges, and a rune-book bound in leather hanging at the hip on a cord or chain. HEAD PIECE (always): an astral diadem on the brow with a central rune-star, hair and face fully visible, never a hat, never a hood. COLOR CONTRAST: vivid matte sky blue #3E86C6 dominant (45-55%), ivory #EFE6D2 under-robe and sleeve linings as a clear light third tone (15-20%), vivid gold #D8A93A runes and trims, only a small magenta #A8326E accent; strong light-dark value contrast, saturated, never a single-tone blue blob. Soft cloth, silk, wool, velvet, brocade, leather straps and thread; no heavy metal. Forbidden: pointed wizard hat, hood over the head, star-and-moon pajama print, gnarled tree-branch staff, all-blue monochrome, navy, indigo, purple or black as the main color, cream-white as the main color, plate armor, breastplate, pauldrons, helmet, chainmail, knight or warrior look, bare muscular arms, short tunic with trousers. `` Forbidden em resumo: chapéu pontudo, capuz, pijama de estrela e lua, cajado de galho retorcido (clichês do mago genérico); monocromia azul, marinho/índigo/roxo/preto dominante (confunde com Necromante e Sombra), creme dominante (confunde com Oráculo); placa, peitoral, ombreira, elmo, malha, cara de guerreiro, braço musculoso à mostra, túnica curta com calça.

MÉTODO (decisão do criador): todo nível, chave ou intermediário, parte do CORPO BASE em pose T (§1.2 homem, §2 mulher). O prompt diz "IGNORE his/her current clothes and dress him/her in a COMPLETELY NEW level N outfit ..." e descreve a roupa inteira do zero (torso, ombros, cinto, saia ou roupão, botas, luvas/braçadeiras, materiais). A continuidade vem da paleta e dos temas no texto. A ARMA parte do cajado do nível anterior, mas é REDESIGNED (cabeça e cabo novos).

Modelo de ramos (do 41 ao 150, padrão final): por nível há ITEM (a armadura neutra, sozinha, fundo carvão), HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A e MULHER-B (o mesmo item interpretado por ramo: Arcanista e Tecelão) e uma ARMA única (cajado que funde orbe e tear). Níveis 1 a 37: HOMEM, MULHER e ARMA completos, partindo do corpo base. A MULHER usa "her", leva a cláusula de corte feminino (FEMALE CUT no 1-15; BULK ONLY IN THE ARMOR PIECES do 21 ao 60) e termina com "Keep her feminine cut." No fim: arte de apresentação (níveis 50 e 150) e os 2 prompts fixos de item.

Regras da cadeia:

  • Todo nível troca peças E materiais: roupão (ou capa), botas e braçadeiras sempre com desenho e material novos; nenhuma peça se repete.
  • Intermediário ≠ chave: ≥2 marcas visíveis a 10 m diferentes do chave (peça de costas, cor de painel/forro, peça de cabeça).
  • Curva de poder: S1 = linho; S2 e S3 sóbrios (lã e tecido liso, sem bordado nem gema); ornamento só da S4 em diante; capa só do 41 em diante (antes, no máximo gola alta ou estola); nunca crânios. Troféus de runista (rune-book, gola, peça de costas, coroa) só crescem, nunca regridem.
  • Sem metal pesado; frente e costas com as mesmas peças; nunca mais leve que o nível anterior (régua de massa: 31); traje fosco (só o 150: brilho muito sutil dentro do bordado).
  • Níveis de forma (60, 80, 100, 130, 150) = "TCHA": SHOWPIECE + acentos vivos (ouro, magenta, marfim); escuro demais reprova.

1. Conceito do piloto masculino S1

1.1 Um conceito (nó "Gerar imagem"; texto no singular; o criador varia pela quantidade de imagens do nó e escolhe 1): `` One stylized fantasy RPG male Mage at level 1, a young rune-scholar of the crystal just starting his journey, Lost Ark / Diablo IV hero quality, simple but striking, full body, front view, standing, plain grey background, neutral light. A tall bell silhouette: an ankle-length sky blue linen robe with a plain bell hem and one thin row of gold rune stitching, huge bell sleeves turned back to show ivory linen linings, a plain standing collar, a short ivory linen stole, a rope belt with a small leather-bound rune-book hanging on a cord, linen wrist wraps, soft leather sandal-boots, a thin plain gold diadem band with one small rune-star on the brow, hair and face clearly visible. He holds a plain pale ash staff with a round wooden knob burned with one gold rune. Vivid matte sky blue #3E86C6 dominant, ivory #EFE6D2 as a clear light third tone, vivid gold #D8A93A thin trim, tiny magenta #A8326E cord accent, strong value contrast, never a single-tone blob. No cape, no metal, no ornament, no pointed hat, no hood, not an old wizard. Matte, saturated colors, no glow, readable silhouette from far away, hand-painted stylized look, no text. ``

1.2 Pose T do escolhido (entrada = o conceito escolhido; é o CORPO BASE masculino de toda a cadeia): `` Same Mage as the input image, same face and body, same S1 apprentice outfit, symmetric T-pose, arms open, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, front view, full body, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no glow. ``

2. Corpo feminino base

Entrada: o piloto masculino em pose T. Esta imagem é o CORPO BASE feminino. `` Same Mage outfit and diadem as the input image, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. Make the character a young woman with a REAL FEMALE BODY, not a slimmed man: a clearly feminine face, a defined bust, a narrow waist cinched by the sky blue sash, rounded hips from which the robe flares into the bell hem, graceful neck and shoulders, long legs, slightly shorter than him. Same apprentice outfit cut for her: the ankle-length sky blue linen robe tailored to her bust and waist, the huge ivory-lined bell sleeves, the short ivory stole, the rope belt with the small rune-book, the thin gold diadem with one small rune-star, long hair visible, soft leather sandal-boots. Elegant and confident, not vulgar. Same palette, matte, no glow. Keep her feminine cut and the same amount of visible skin. ``

3. Cabeças

3.1 Prompt da cabeça (entrada = o corpo vestido; trocar o trecho entre colchetes pela descrição de 3.2): `` Head and bare neck only of this character: [DESCRIÇÃO DA CABEÇA]. Same thin gold astral diadem with one small star on the brow (always worn), no pointed hat, no veil, stylized fantasy face, not photorealistic, front view, plain grey background, neutral light, no shoulders, no clothing, no glow. ``

3.2 Descrições (3 + 3, todas distintas; o diadema fica):

  • M1: older man, long straight silver hair and a long silver beard, calm grey eyes, deep lines at the brow.
  • M2: man in his thirties, medium wavy dark brown hair with one lock dyed in a star shape, sharp cheekbones, curious bright eyes.
  • M3: young man, short spiky black hair with one thin blue thread braided in, round face, freckles, wide eager eyes.
  • F1: young woman, long loose curls of chestnut hair, soft features, amber eyes, small beauty mark.
  • F2: woman in her thirties, a very long side braid with sky blue ribbons woven in, high cheekbones, green eyes.
  • F3: young woman, pixie cut with one pale celeste lock over the brow, pointed chin, large grey eyes.

4. Mãos

`` Male hand pair, both hands only, relaxed open fingers, palms down, same skin tone as the Mage, a linen wrist wrap at each wrist, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no ring, no weapon, no glow. ` ` Female hand pair, both hands only, slender relaxed open fingers, palms down, same skin tone as the female Mage, a linen wrist wrap at each wrist, plain grey background, neutral light, matte, no ring, no weapon, no glow. ``

5. Cadeia de níveis (1 ao 150)

Entradas: HOMEM = corpo base masculino em pose T (§1.2); MULHER = corpo base feminino em pose T (§2); ARMA = o cajado do nível anterior (nível 1: o do conceito escolhido), sempre REDESIGNED. Ordem: 1, 6, 11, 15, 21, 26, 31, 37 (HOMEM, MULHER e ARMA completos); do 41 ao 150, modelo de ramos por nível: ITEM neutro, HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A, MULHER-B e uma ARMA única.

Nível 1 · S1 · Linho de Aprendiz (piloto)

Ideia: simples mas marcante; o sino azul e marfim já se lê de longe. Parte de: corpo base. HOMEM `` Same Mage as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level 1 outfit. MAGE IDENTITY (never lose this): a young rune-scholar of the crystal, elegant, sharp and arcane, NOT a generic old wizard. SILHOUETTE: a tall bell: an ankle-length robe flaring into a wide bell hem, HUGE bell sleeves far wider than the elbows and a tall stiff standing collar framing the head. SIGNATURE: gold runes stitched along the hem and sleeve edges, and a rune-book bound in leather hanging at the hip on a cord or chain. HEAD PIECE (always): an astral diadem on the brow with a central rune-star, hair and face fully visible, never a hat, never a hood. COLOR CONTRAST: vivid matte sky blue #3E86C6 dominant (45-55%), ivory #EFE6D2 under-robe and sleeve linings as a clear light third tone (15-20%), vivid gold #D8A93A runes and trims, only a small magenta #A8326E accent; strong light-dark value contrast, saturated, never a single-tone blue blob. Soft cloth, silk, wool, velvet, brocade, leather straps and thread; no heavy metal. Forbidden: pointed wizard hat, hood over the head, star-and-moon pajama print, gnarled tree-branch staff, all-blue monochrome, navy, indigo, purple or black as the main color, cream-white as the main color, plate armor, breastplate, pauldrons, helmet, chainmail, knight or warrior look, bare muscular arms, short tunic with trousers. OUTFIT: a simple apprentice robe: an ankle-length sky blue linen robe with a plain bell hem and one thin row of gold rune stitching at the hem, wide bell sleeves turned back to show ivory linen linings, a plain standing collar, a short ivory linen stole, a rope belt with a small leather-bound rune-book hanging on a cord, linen wrist wraps, soft leather sandal-boots, a thin plain gold diadem band with one small rune-star on the brow. Simple but striking: few pieces, a clean bell silhouette, a clear blue-and-ivory contrast. No cape, no gems, no ornament, no pointed hat, matte, no glow. ` MULHER ` Same Mage as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. IGNORE her current clothes and dress her in a COMPLETELY NEW level 1 outfit. MAGE IDENTITY (never lose this): a young rune-scholar of the crystal, elegant, sharp and arcane, NOT a generic old wizard. FEMALE CUT: a real woman's silhouette, not a slimmed man: the robe is tailored to her bust, a sash cinches a clearly narrow waist and the skirt flares from her hips into the bell hem; graceful neck and shoulders, the same big sleeves and collar as the male version, elegant, not vulgar. Keep her feminine cut. ` ARMA ` Same staff as the input image, REDESIGNED for level 1 (new head AND new shaft): NEW head: a plain round carved wooden knob with one gold rune burned into it. NEW shaft: a straight pale ash pole with one sky blue cord wrapped below the knob. Taller, more elaborate and more striking than the previous level. Full length, vertical, side view, plain light grey background, no hands, no glow. ``

Nível 15 · S2 · Lã Reforçada (sóbrio)

ITEM `` ITEM: Concept art of ONE Mage robe-and-cape set laid out flat as an inventory item, no body, no weapon, no hands, plain dark charcoal background, front view, matte. Level 60: a grand sky blue silk cape in three tiers lined in ivory and magenta, its hem set with small obsidian shards, a towering astral collar, a sky blue robe with ivory bell sleeves and gold rune bands, a broad gold rune-chain, obsidian-shard bracers in gold settings, boots with gold filigree shafts, a tall crown of crystal and gold. Sky blue #3E86C6 dominant with ivory #EFE6D2 linings, vivid gold #D8A93A runes and a small magenta #A8326E accent, soft cloth, matte, no glow, no pointed hat. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. ``

Ramo A · Cristalomante (HOMEM-A e MULHER-A)

Ideia: o Cristalomante carrega cristal no corpo: coroa de cristais nos ombros, halo de orrery gigante; obsidiana só como acento. HOMEM-A `` Same Mage as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level 60 outfit, the Cristalomante interpretation of the level 60 item. MAGE IDENTITY (never lose this): a young rune-scholar of the crystal, elegant, sharp and arcane, NOT a generic old wizard. SILHOUETTE: a tall bell: an ankle-length robe flaring into a wide bell hem, HUGE bell sleeves far wider than the elbows and a tall stiff standing collar framing the head. SIGNATURE: gold runes stitched along the hem and sleeve edges, and a rune-book bound in leather hanging at the hip on a cord or chain. HEAD PIECE (always): an astral diadem on the brow with a central rune-star, hair and face fully visible, never a hat, never a hood. COLOR CONTRAST: vivid matte sky blue #3E86C6 dominant (45-55%), ivory #EFE6D2 under-robe and sleeve linings as a clear light third tone (15-20%), vivid gold #D8A93A runes and trims, only a small magenta #A8326E accent; strong light-dark value contrast, saturated, never a single-tone blue blob. Soft cloth, silk, wool, velvet, brocade, leather straps and thread; no heavy metal. Forbidden: pointed wizard hat, hood over the head, star-and-moon pajama print, gnarled tree-branch staff, all-blue monochrome, navy, indigo, purple or black as the main color, cream-white as the main color, plate armor, breastplate, pauldrons, helmet, chainmail, knight or warrior look, bare muscular arms, short tunic with trousers. OUTFIT: a grand three-tier sky blue silk cape to the ground lined in ivory and magenta, its hem set with small black obsidian shards, tall matte pale-blue crystal shards rising from the shoulder collar like a crown of crystal, a huge gold orrery halo standing behind the head with orbiting crystal and obsidian spheres, a sky blue robe with huge ivory bell sleeves banded in gold runes, the rune-tome bound in gold on a chain, bracers set with obsidian shards in gold settings, tall boots with gold filigree shafts, a tall crown of crystal and gold around the central rune-star. No heavy metal, no pointed hat, matte, no glow. ARCH-MAGE ACCENTS (make it vivid, not dark): vivid gold rune bands and star finials on the collar and the back piece, a broad gold rune-chain across the chest, a strong magenta silk sash and lining flashing at the edges, bright ivory linings against the sky blue, obsidian only as small dark accents. SHOWPIECE: this is the first legendary milestone robe (form level), the most imposing and epic Mage look so far, an instant 'wow' at first sight: a bigger, more dramatic silhouette than any earlier level, a towering collar and a grand back piece rising behind the shoulders, layered silk, velvet and gold, rich hand-crafted detail everywhere, still matte, no glow. CRYSTAL TOUCH (incredibly subtle): only one or two tiny matte pale-blue crystal flecks embedded somewhere in the gear, almost unnoticeable at first glance, no glow. ` MULHER-A ` Same Mage as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon, plain grey background, neutral light. SIGNATURE: gold runes stitched along the hem and sleeve edges, and a rune-book bound in leather hanging at the hip on a cord or chain. HEAD PIECE (always): an astral diadem on the brow with a central rune-star, hair and face fully visible, never a hat, never a hood. COLOR CONTRAST: vivid matte sky blue #3E86C6 dominant (45-55%), ivory #EFE6D2 under-robe and sleeve linings as a clear light third tone (15-20%), vivid gold #D8A93A runes and trims, only a small magenta #A8326E accent; strong light-dark value contrast, saturated, never a single-tone blue blob. Soft cloth, silk, wool, velvet, brocade, leather straps and thread; no heavy metal. Forbidden: pointed wizard hat, hood over the head, star-and-moon pajama print, gnarled tree-branch staff, all-blue monochrome, navy, indigo, purple or black as the main color, cream-white as the main color, plate armor, breastplate, pauldrons, helmet, chainmail, knight or warrior look, bare muscular arms, short tunic with trousers. The heaviest so far, no heavy metal, no pointed hat, matte, no glow. 6. Arte de apresentação (Mago: nível 50 e nível 150)

Duas versões de arte de capa e retrato, uma no nível 50 e outra no nível 150. Entrada: as imagens aprovadas de HOMEM e MULHER do nível indicado, do mesmo ramo (se quiser as duas variantes, repetir com o outro ramo). O nível 50 usa o traje do nível 48, o último vestido ao chegar nos 50; o nível 150 usa o 150-A ou o 150-B. Anexar também a arma aprovada do nível.

Versão nível 50

KEY ART (anexar HOMEM e MULHER juntos) `` Epic key art for the Mage class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (male and female) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their staves topped with a crystal orb in gold prongs wrapped in gold thread in powerful poses, an arcane observatory with a great dome open to the stars, floating glass spheres, star charts and looms hung with gold thread, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO (um por personagem: HOMEM e MULHER, um de cada vez) ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. Three-quarter view, confident look, dramatic rim light, dark charcoal background, painterly game portrait style, high detail, square 1:1, no text. ``

Versão nível 150

KEY ART (anexar HOMEM e MULHER juntos) `` Epic key art for the Mage class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (male and female) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their prestige staves with a great crystal head, a second crystal halo and gold filigree in powerful poses, the same observatory dome at night under a vast sky, a huge halo ring of cut crystal and a great comet of gold thread crossing the stars, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO (um por personagem: HOMEM e MULHER, um de cada vez) ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. Three-quarter view, confident look, dramatic rim light, dark charcoal background, painterly game portrait style, high detail, square 1:1, no text. ``

7. Prompts FIXOS de arte do item (fundo carvão)

Entrada: a imagem do traje aprovado (para ARMADURA) ou da arma aprovada (para ARMA; vale também para o livro, o escudo e a lança). Os mesmos dois prompts valem para todo nível; nunca editar.

ARMADURA: `` The full torso armor from the input image displayed alone as a game item, including ALL its parts: collar or cape, both pauldrons, chest pieces and straps, belt and the full skirt. No boots, no bracers, no gloves, no arms, no body. Front view, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail. ` ARMA: ` The same weapon from the input image displayed alone as a game item: identical design, materials and colors. Diagonal angle from bottom-left to top-right, centered, plain dark charcoal background, soft studio light from the top-left, subtle rim light, high detail, no hands, no text. ``

Ragna Tale é um nome de trabalho; pode mudar antes do lançamento.
Jogo em desenvolvimento. Imagens são conceitos de design, não capturas do jogo final. Sem cookies e sem rastreadores.
